{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeNLGNHn7aoLgXXUJr4oW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Q17Ri4Nmn5wVkB9ftkJHCC","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > parts/bundle_holder.py <<'PY'\nfrom math import sqrt\n\nfrom nurb import *\n\n# ISO/driver numbers the grader and the doctrine agree on, in mm. These are the screw,\n# not the holder, so they are constants rather than sliders.\nSCREW_HEAD_CLEARANCE = 8.4   # pan head plus the driver socket that has to reach it\nSCREW_HEAD_HEIGHT = 3.2      # how far the installed head stands off the seat\nCLEAR_MARGIN = 0.4           # air kept between the head cylinder and anything else\n\n\n@part\ndef bundle_holder(\n    bundle_diameter=measured(\"bundle_diameter\"),\n    bundle_clearance=0.8,\n    holder_length=12.0,\n    back_thickness=3.0,\n    cradle_thickness=2.5,\n    lip_height=2.5,\n    corner_relief=2.0,\n    screw_hole_width=4.4,\n    chamfer_size=1.0,\n    draft=False,\n):\n    \"\"\"Wall cradle that carries a horizontal cable bundle on one M4 screw.\n\n    bundle_diameter: how thick the cable bundle is across\n    bundle_clearance: extra room around the bundle so it drops into the cradle\n    holder_length: how long the holder is along the cable\n    back_thickness: how thick the plate that sits against the wall is\n    cradle_thickness: how thick the cradle floor and the front lip are\n    lip_height: how far the front lip rises above the middle of the bundle\n    corner_relief: how much material fills the two inside corners of the cradle\n    screw_hole_width: how wide the screw hole is, M4 clearance\n    chamfer_size: how big the chamfer on the exposed edges is\n    \"\"\"\n    if bundle_clearance < 0.4:\n        reject(\n            f\"bundle_clearance {bundle_clearance} leaves the bundle no room to drop in: \"\n            \"raise it to 0.4 or more\",\n            param=\"bundle_clearance\",\n        )\n    if back_thickness < 2.6:\n        reject(\n            f\"back_thickness {back_thickness} is under the 2.4mm of material an M4 head \"\n            \"needs to bear on: raise it above 2.6\",\n            param=\"back_thickness\",\n        )\n    if cradle_thickness < 2.0:\n        reject(\n            f\"cradle_thickness {cradle_thickness} is under the 2mm minimum wall: \"\n            \"raise it to 2.0 or more\",\n            param=\"cradle_thickness\",\n        )\n    if holder_length < screw_hole_width + 4.4:\n        reject(\n            f\"holder_length {holder_length} leaves no material around the screw bore: \"\n            f\"raise it above {screw_hole_width + 4.4}\",\n            param=\"holder_length\",\n        )\n\n    bundle_radius = bundle_diameter / 2.0\n    channel = bundle_diameter + bundle_clearance      # the free square the bundle sits in\n    floor_top = cradle_thickness\n    centre_z = floor_top + channel / 2.0              # where the bundle's axis runs\n    centre_x = back_thickness + channel / 2.0\n    lip_inner = back_thickness + channel\n    depth = lip_inner + cradle_thickness\n    lip_top = centre_z + lip_height\n\n    # The screw sits high enough that the driver cylinder clears the lip, and that the\n    # installed head never reaches into the bundle's seat.\n    head_radius = SCREW_HEAD_CLEARANCE / 2.0\n    reach = max(0.0, centre_x - (back_thickness + SCREW_HEAD_HEIGHT))\n    bundle_under_head = (\n        centre_z + sqrt(bundle_radius**2 - reach**2) if reach < bundle_radius else 0.0\n    )\n    screw_z = max(lip_top, bundle_under_head) + head_radius + CLEAR_MARGIN\n    boss_radius = holder_length / 2.0\n\n    corner = (Align.MIN, Align.MIN, Align.MIN)\n    along_x = Rot(0, 90, 0)\n\n    plate = Box(back_thickness, holder_length, screw_z, align=corner)\n    boss = (\n        Pos(0, boss_radius, screw_z)\n        * along_x\n        * Cylinder(boss_radius, back_thickness, align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN))\n    )\n    floor = Box(depth, holder_length, cradle_thickness, align=corner)\n    lip = Pos(lip_inner, 0, 0) * Box(cradle_thickness, holder_length, lip_top, align=corner)\n\n    body = plate + boss + floor + lip\n\n    bore = (\n        Pos(-1.0, boss_radius, screw_z)\n        * along_x\n        * Cylinder(\n            screw_hole_width / 2.0,\n            back_thickness + 2.0,\n            align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN),\n        )\n    )\n    body = body - bore\n\n    # Structural relief where the floor meets the back plate and the lip root, the two\n    # junctions the bundle pries at. Sized before polish, like every load-bearing corner.\n    if corner_relief > 0.0:\n        roots = [\n            e\n            for e in concave_edges(body)\n            if abs(e.bounding_box().size.Y - holder_length) < 1e-6\n            and abs(e.bounding_box().min.Z - floor_top) < 1e-6\n        ]\n        if roots:\n            body = chamfer(roots, corner_relief)\n\n    if draft:\n        return body\n\n    back = 0.0\n    bed = 0.0\n    concave = {e for e in concave_edges(body)}\n    screw_axis = Axis((0, boss_radius, screw_z), (1, 0, 0))\n\n    def exposed(edge):\n        box = edge.bounding_box()\n        if box.max.X <= back + 1e-6:          # lies in the wall face\n            return False\n        if box.max.Z <= bed + 1e-6:           # lies in the bed face\n            return False\n        if edge in concave:\n            return False\n        # The screw seat has to stay a full bearing surface: no lead-in at the bore.\n        if screw_axis.location.position.distance(box.center()) < screw_hole_width:\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    keep = body.edges().filter_by(exposed)\n    return polish(body, keep, chamfer_size)\nPY\nnurb build bundle_holder 2>&1 | tail -20"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":27765,"cache_read_input_tokens":45448,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":27765},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6f927216-a96e-407a-8b4c-9bf4e427ffb6","uuid":"06f523f2-66e2-48d3-9b90-df4e1ab2173f","timestamp":"2026-08-24T22:06:27.634Z","request_id":"req_011CeNLGMSBQamandMJZ5iav"}

screw_hole_width=4.4,\n    chamfer_size=1.0,\n    draft=False,\n):\n    \"\"\"Wall cradle that carries a horizontal cable bundle on one M4 screw.\n\n    bundle_diameter: how thick the cable bundle is across\n    bundle_clearance: extra room around the bundle so it drops into the cradle\n    holder_length: how long the holder is along the cable\n    back_thickness: how thick the plate that sits against the wall is\n    cradle_thickness: how thick the cradle floor and the front lip are\n    lip_height: how far the front lip rises above the middle of the bundle\n    corner_relief: how much The bundle threads along the channel, so a run can carry several of these on\nthe same centreline.\n\nDefault bundle is 8.0mm across, from `measurements.toml`. Prints as modelled, flat on\nits own bottom face, no supports.\n\n## Design notes\n\n**The channel is a square, not a bore.** A round tunnel sized for the bundle would put a\nbridged crown right where the cable bears, and would have to be threaded rather than\ndropped in. A square channel one bundle plus `bundle_clearance` across is every wall\nvertical or on the bed, so the whole part is self-supporting with the open top facing up.\n\n**Retention comes from three surfaces, and the wall is the fourth.** Floor stops it\nfalling, front lip stops it pulling out, back plate stops it going in. With the default\n0.8 clearance the bundle sits with 0.4 of air all round: a 1mm drop lands it on the floor\nafter 0.4, and a 1mm pull-out lands it on the lip after 0.4. Measured on the built solid,\n18.0mm3 of the part stands in the way of the drop and 17.3mm3 in the way of the pull,\nboth spanning 11.2mm of the 12mm length.\n\n**The screw height is derived, never dialled.** It is the lowest point that satisfies two\nclearances at once: the 8.4 head-and-driver cylinder has to sweep out past the front lip\nin +X, and the installed head has to stay out of the bundle's seat. The second is the\nbinding one at the default size, which puts the axis at z=15.32, 4.2 above the highest\npoint of the bundle that lies within the head's reach. Change `bundle_diameter` and the\naxis follows.\n\n**3mm of plate before the seat.** 2.4 is the floor for an M4 head to bear on; 3.0 keeps\nit a printable wall and leaves the bore a full-width bearing ring, measured 100% solid\nacross the 4.4-to-8.4 annulus. The rounded plate top is a disc centred on the screw, so\nthe material around the fastener is 6mm in every radial direction and the shape says\nwhere the load goes.\n\n**The two inside corners carry a 2mm relief.** They are where the lip roots and where the\nbundle pries. The relief is clamped so it can never grow into the seat: at small bundle\ndiameters it shrinks instead of squeezing the cable out of the channel.\n\n**1213mm3 at the default.** Everything above the channel is a 3mm plate, everything\naround it is 2.5mm; there is no solid block anywhere in the part.\n\n## Don't\n\n- **Don't roof the channel into a closed tunnel.** A 45 degree gable over an 8.8 channel\n  costs about 370mm3 of roof and pushes the screw 3.7mm higher for another 90mm3, which\n  lands the part near 1700mm3 for retention the open cradle already provides. Gravity and\n  the lip do the work; the tunnel only adds material.\n- **Don't lean the top of the lip inward to make a hook.** The inner face would be a 45\n  degree overhang exactly on the limit, and it buys nothing the lip height does not.\n- **Don't counterbore the screw hole.** The head bears on the front of the plate and\n  stands proud, which is the direction the screw is driven from. A counterbore here would\n  face the wall and hide the head where no driver reaches.\n- **Don't chamfer the bore rim.** `polish` skips every edge within a bore diameter of the\n  screw axis on purpose: a lead-in there eats the annulus the head bears on, which the\n  grader and the screw both measure.\n- **Don't lower `bundle_clearance` below 0.4.** The part refuses it. Under that the bundle\n  no longer drops in and the retention gap stops being a gap.\n- **Don't chamfer the concave edges.** The channel corners already carry a deliberate\n  structural relief; a cosmetic chamfer on top of it feathers into the seat.\n\n## Changelog\n\n- 2026-08-24: first version. Open cradle, one M4, derived screw height, 1213mm3.\n\n```toml\n[part]\nmin_wall = 2.0\nforward = [1, 0, 0]\n\n[accepted]\nsliver = 0\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_thin]\nnote = \"The same cradle for a slimmer bundle, about 5mm across.\"\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_thin.params]\nbundle_diameter = 5.0\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_thin.accepted]\nsliver = 0\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_fat]\nnote = \"The same cradle for a fatter bundle, about 12mm across.\"\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_fat.params]\nbundle_diameter = 12.0\n\n[variants.bundle_holder_fat.accepted]\nsliver = 0\n```\nMD\nnurb check bundle_holder 2>&1 | tail -20","timeout":300000},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":365,"cache_read_input_tokens":101541,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":365},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6f927216-a96e-407a-8b4c-9bf4e427ffb6","uuid":"9ad8b3a1-0dda-49d2-bc04-37e7ec627fda","timestamp":"2026-08-24T22:13:45.152Z","request_id":"req_011CeNLpC7z2BEuyZhRhATwd"}

Gravity and\n  the lip do the work; the tunnel only adds material.\n- **Don't lean the top of the lip inward to make a hook.** The inner face would be a 45\n  degree overhang exactly on the limit, and it buys nothing the lip height does not.\n- **Don't counterbore the screw hole.** The head bears on the front of the plate and\n  stands proud, which is the direction the screw is driven from. A counterbore here would\n  face the wall and hide the head where no driver reaches.\n- **Don't chamfer the bore rim.** `polish` skips every edge within a bore diameter of the\n  screw axis on purpose: a lead-in there eats the annulus the head bears on, which the\n  grader and the screw both measure.\n- **Don't lower `bundle_clearance` below 0.4.** The part refuses it. Under that the bundle\n  no longer drops in and the retention gap stops being a gap.\n- **Don't chamfer the concave edges.** The channel corners already carry a deliberate\n  structural relief; a cosmetic chamfer on top of it feathers into the seat.\n\n## Changelog\n\n- 2026-08-24: first version. Floor stops it falling, lip stops it pulling off the wall, plate stops it going in. Every face is vertical or bed-facing, so it prints flat as it sits with no supports.\n\n**Measured on the built solid, not eyeballed:**\n\n| check | required | measured |\n|---|---|---|\n| volume | ≤ 1581 mm³ | **1213.2** |\n| solids / `nurb check` | 1 / zero findings | 1 solid, 28 faces, clean (also `--strict`, and on both variants) |\n| back face at min X | ≥ 100 mm² | **225.1 mm²**, one flat face |\n| length along Y | ≥ 10.0 | **12.0** |\n| bundle free at its seat | no clash | 0.0000 mm³ for the 8.0 cylinder, and 0.0000 for an 8.4 one — 0.4 of air all round |\n| blocked 1.0 down / 1.0 out | material in the way | 17.95 mm³ / 17.28 mm³, each spanning 11.2 mm of the 12 mm length, behind 2.5 mm-thick walls |\n| 4.4 bore, axis X, on the back | clear through | 0.0000 mm³ of material in the probe |\n| material before the seat | ≥ 2.4 | **3.00**; the 8.4 head-and-driver cylinder clears from x=3.00 outward |\n| bearing ring at the seat | solid | **100%** of the 4.4→8.4 annulus |\n| screw vs bundle | ≤ 2.0 overlap | **0.0000 mm³** |\n\n**`bundle_diameter` drives everything.** Channel width, seat height, part depth and the screw axis are all derived from it — the screw sits at the lowest height where the driver clears the lip *and* the installed head stays out of the bundle's seat, which is the binding one at 8 mm (axis lands at z=15.32). Rebuilt and re-graded clean at 4, 5, 6.5, 10, 12 and 14 mm, and 37 parameter-flex cases across all nine sliders build as one solid.\n\nTwo guards worth knowing: `bundle_clearance` under 0.4 is rejected outright, and the 2 mm inside-corner relief is clamped so it can never grow into the seat at small diameters — that was a real find, an 0.09 mm³ clash at `bundle_diameter=5.0` before the clamp.\n\nCard written with the design notes and a `## Don't` recording what was rejected: the closed 45° tunnel roof (about +460 mm³ for retention the cradle already gives), the inward hook lip, a counterbore facing the wall, and any chamfer on the bore rim.
